<input type='checkbox' name='article_1592' value='https://finance.yahoo.com/video/most-families-retirement-wrong-190000912.html' /> 1592 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=finance.yahoo.com+How+most+families+get+retirement+wrongConventional+wisdom+can+only+get+you+so+far%2C+especially+when+dealing+with+unforeseen+life+events+that+might+permanently+change+your+plans.+On+this+week%27s+episode+of+Decoding+Retirement%2C+host+Robert+%22Bob%22+Powell+speaks+with+Keith+Wargo%2C+president+and+CEO+of+Autism+Speaks%2C+about+the+financial+necessities+of+autism+care.+Keith+discusses+resources+to+help+families+affected+by+autism%2C+the+importance+of+setting+up+special+needs+trusts%2C+and+ways+to+stay+on+track+for+retirement.+Later+in+the+episode%2C+Bob+chats+with+Zvi+Bodie%2C+Boston+University+professor+of+management+emeritus%2C+about+why+the+risks+inherent+in+buying+stocks+may+be+higher+than+you+think.+Tune+in+now+for+two+important+conversations+on+one+eye-opening+episode+of+Decoding+Retirement.Yahoo+Finance%27s+Decoding+Retirement+is+hosted+by+Robert+Powell.+Find+more+episodes+of+Decoding+Retirement+at+https%3A%2F%2Ffinance.yahoo.com%2Fvideos%2Fseries%2Fdecoding-retirement.' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://finance.yahoo.com/video/most-families-retirement-wrong-190000912.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How most families get retirement wrongConventional wisdom can only get you so far, especially when dealing with unforeseen life events that might permanently change your plans. On this week's episode of Decoding Retirement, host Robert "Bob" Powell speaks with Keith Wargo, president and CEO of Autism Speaks, about the financial necessities of autism care. Keith discusses resources to help families affected by autism, the importance of setting up special needs trusts, and ways to stay on track for retirement. Later in the episode, Bob chats with Zvi Bodie, Boston University professor of management emeritus, about why the risks inherent in buying stocks may be higher than you think. Tune in now for two important conversations on one eye-opening episode of Decoding Retirement.Yahoo Finance's Decoding Retirement is hosted by Robert Powell. Find more episodes of Decoding Retirement at https://finance.yahoo.com/videos/series/decoding-retirement.</a><br>
